## LRS (learned-rule search) - NQ - library used: lrs_lib (ZL-1, find/lrs_lib.py), NOT instr2/lrs.py (BASE-4)

**NQ (generic-grid control) (learned-rule search, lrs_lib): the thing a small learned rule can find is nothing beyond the null; the book that fits is none; it does not clear because the best of 648 candidates (all learners, horizons, sessions, thresholds, brackets, 3 seeds) has joint z 2.48 against a family-null maximum with median 2.47 and 95th percentile 3.20 (p_family 0.502), and 0 candidates have p_family <= 0.005 with both windows positive.**

Grid: 648 candidates (3 learners x 3 horizons x 4 sessions x 2 q x 3 brackets x 3 seeds), purged walk-forward 24 months, embargo 2 x horizon, OOS from 2023-04, ALLINST hash worlds W=200, windows L6M 2026-05-01..10-31 and HOLD 2025-11-01..10-31; dead_lookup: 648 of 648 exact-spec hits flagged (ARTIFACT: the spec string had no instrument key, so rows appended by the YM/6E runs matched; flags never remove cells, nothing was skipped; later runs key on instrument); ledger rows appended (program LRS); n_tested registered 648. Walk-forward = instr2_eval.walk_forward over the same learner / horizon / session neighbourhood (q x bracket x seed, up to 18 variants), monthly choice by trailing 6 / 12-month Calmar, 2-day purge, OOS 2024-01..2026-10, carry dropped. The $ columns are for the top-z finalist, equal $1,200 risk.

**Pooled-table caveats (ZL-1):** (1) BTC and NKD show z 0.00 and a null of 0: their max_lots_per_5min in instr2_sessions.json is 0 in the tested sessions, so lots clip to 0, every world has zero dollars and the candidates are degenerate (no tradable size under the 1.7%-of-volume cap; consistent with the CAP-BREACH flag), not evidence for or against a rule. Their pooled-table rows and the 13-instrument joint p are therefore over 11 live instruments. (2) A "+0 / +0" walk-forward means the selector stayed flat every month (no variant had a positive trailing mean). (3) Only GC has a positive walk-forward on both lookbacks (+111 / +181 $/wk); its top finalist has PRE -3,876 $/wk, own-instrument p_family 0.264, so it is no lead. (4) The four non-keyed ledger batches are described in l2vast/ds3/out/uf/DEAD_CELLS_NOTES.md.

**NKD: the thing it does is follow NQ with a one-minute lag inside a Tokyo-hours range (47% of the day's range in 18:00-21:00 ET), bounce from prior-day high/lows (46-51% vs 41-44% null) and fade excursions from the session open; the book that fits is a prior-day-level / 3 ATR5 excursion fade in Asia and NY-AM, but the 1.7% cap is 0.2-0.8 contracts per 5 minutes (CAP-BREACH) and the round trip is $130; it does not clear because the screen's best family p is 0.26, the Asia cells that look good (L1M..HOLD +110 to +302 per week at uncapped lots, MID +549 to +653) are +6/-11 in PRE and -296/-335 in X' with 0 of 10 pre-2023 years positive, p_book 0.42-0.48, and the learned rules sit at p_family 0.87.**

## DS3_FIND CHAR-C method notes and limits (2026-11-05T23:44Z)
Prereg: l2vast/ds3/prereg/FIND_C_scope.md sha256 ad66f853047872749f2f86a62cff51ddaa826f14e2fbaeed54a8663ed0258a5d + addendum 1 sha256 1946ce6e734e9755ef9b717c00ef58ab12d6be83bc264fcadebae3f1904e0ac8 (DECISIONS.md, before any book was scored); map = CHAR-A char_lib.py sha256 844e499f14c5d6821d3776c88e97a078bb2a27ff628e43f391d9c019c2423c27 on the CHAR-C bad-print-repaired bars; map->book rule frozen by CHAR-A (p_family <= .05 AND room_A > 2.0). Stage 1 = 1-minute bar simulator with the Z2 slips (a screen: resting targets fill with zero slip, so it is MORE generous than the evaluator); family null = random-side re-simulation of EVERY cell (100 worlds, z = min(L3M, L6M, HOLD)/MDE_L6M; per-world max vectors saved /mnt/l2/l2vast/find_c/nullmax_<SYM>.npy). Stage 2 = instr2_eval.evaluate (evaluator of record: full round-trip cost on every trade, shift5 forward-only / random-side / shuffled-day placebos x100, own-parameter purged walk-forward over the same book's other cells, corr and stack effect V0/V2, frames in /mnt/l2/l2vast/instr2_trades/<SYM>/FIND_*.parquet). $/wk at equal-$1,200-risk integer lots, carry weeks dropped; 'pre-2023 yrs R>0' = deep 2013+ out-of-sample in R units with costs scaled by price (percent frame; stage-1 simulator, so generous). Look-ahead: truncation test of the cc_book builders 0 mismatches in 160 checks (MBT/6E/NKD, 4-8 random cuts x 8 signal sets). Ledger: dead_lookup before (5 representative specs, no EXACT/NEAR hits), append_cells after (programs FIND_C_<SYM>, 1,812 rows; FIND_C_LRSLIB_MBT/NKD 1,134 rows); n_tested registered for CHAR-C: 6E 588, 6J 480, NKD 132, MBT 288, BTC 324 (+ lrs_lib MBT 810, NKD 324). Shadow only, no PASS claims, nothing live.
Limits: (1) stage-1 screen vs evaluator: the screen fills resting targets at zero slip and charges slips only on stops/time exits; the evaluator charges the full rt_market_in_stop_out on every trade, so screen numbers are generous (6J pre-NY cell: screen L6M +86, evaluator -30 per week) and the family null is a screen null; (2) 1-minute bars only (no 1-s tape for 6E/6J/NKD/MBT on this box); (3) 6E/6J 2013-2015 deep-OOS years use repaired bars (cc_clean.py) - the originals are contaminated; (4) three signal builders (vwap_band, sess_excursion, on_levels) skip days whose bar count is below 30/20/60 - a data-completeness filter evaluated on the whole session; the truncation tests (160 checks) found no mismatch; (5) MBT/BTC history starts 2022-01 (4.8 years; the close-session MBT history is sparse before 2024), so X' and 'pre-2023' are partly or wholly missing for crypto; (6) NKD/BTC cells use equal-$ lots WITHOUT the participation cap (CAP-BREACH, not executable at that size); (7) MBT learned-rule cells used BASE-4 lrs.py (120 candidates in the MBT family) and lrs_lib (810); (8) the h=1 map rows were mapped to 5-minute decisions (signals are 5-minute bucket closes). Map-only findings (no book, rate or descriptive rows): the 6E EUR drift at 07:30-08:00 ET (-0.8 bp, 0.48 x cost), 6E/6J/NKD gap-fill rates, VR/Hurst, day-type shares, NQ lead-lag (1-minute, <= 0.14 corr), crypto weekend structure (none).

## DS3_FIND plain sentences (CHAR-A, ES RTY YM + NQ control; map 2026-11-05, Step 2 books from map rows with p_family<=.05 and room_A>2x; all Shadow, no PASS)
* ES: the thing it does is revert at 5-15 min in every session except the 09:30-12:00 open drive (5-min autocorr -0.016 to -0.038, RTH 30-min variance ratio 0.915 vs null 1.000) and trend only in the last 15:00-16:55 half hour (+0.052 at 30 min); the book that fits is a 5-30 min fade with the stop beyond the window extreme and a 50-100% retrace target, plus overnight-high bounces at 12:00-16:55; it does NOT clear because the edge is far smaller than the 0.85-point round-trip cost (730 cells, 50 positive in L6M+HOLD, 15 tolerant, best family p 0.78; the best ES London 120-min fade is a 2026 regime, negative in 2013-2022 except 2020).
* RTY: the thing it does is revert strongly in the thin 08:30-09:30 pre-market (30-min autocorr -0.10) and at 5 min in asia/london, with no tradable regularity in RTH (variance ratio 0.99, no level beats its null); the book that fits is the pre-market 30-min fade; it does not clear (396 cells + 54 learned-rule cells, 0 cleared, best family p 0.38; the +$500/wk L6M cells have 7-14 trades, fail PRE/X', and the 2018-2025 percent-frame years are negative).
* YM: the thing it does is 5-15 min reversion in asia/london/pre/nypm/close, near random walk in 09:30-12:00 (Hurst 0.504, no level beats its null); the book that fits is a 5-min fade outside the open; it does not clear (557 + 72 cells, 0 cleared, best family p 0.15).
* NQ (control): near random walk with a 60-min trend in asia/nypm and a 30-min trend in the close (+0.047); only the Asian-session prior-day-level break-through beats the level null. No NQ book was built (control only).
Cross-instrument: ES/RTY/YM correlate 0.6-0.9 with NQ at 1-60 min; 1-min lead/lag with NQ is below |0.015| (statistically real, economically nil). Honest caveats: 1-min bars only (no tick path); structure-family nulls use 60-100 shuffled-day worlds (p resolution ~0.016); many hour-level AC cells are small-sample; 1-min autocorrelation is bid-ask bounce.

## DS3_FIND MBT - pre-stated seed replication of the lrs_lib close-session momentum cell (CHAR-C, addendum 2 sha256 in DECISIONS.md, filed before these cells were scored)
Configuration (tree_mom, 60-minute horizon, close session 15:00-16:55 ET, q .85, brackets atr2/1.5R, atr3/2R, atr3/none). Original run (seeds 0-2): seed 2 joint z 4.8 (p_family .005), seed 0 3.1 (.045), seed 1 0.8-1.7 (1.0). Replication, MBT seeds 3-7 (15 cells, own-run hash-world null W=200): z_L > 0 and z_H > 0 in all five seeds and all three brackets, joint z by seed (median of the three brackets) 0.5 (seed 4), 2.4 (seed 3), 1.8 (seed 5), 1.6 (seed 6), 2.8 (seed 7); median joint z 1.80; criterion stated beforehand = median >= 2.0 -> NOT MET. BTC standard (same underlying, CAP-BREACH), seeds 0-4: z_L, z_H > 0 in all five seeds, median joint z 1.44 -> NOT MET. Reading: the sign of the 60-minute close-session momentum rule replicates in 13 of 13 new seeds (the learner's side beats random sides in both windows), the size does not reach the pre-stated bar, the seeds are not independent (70% subsamples of one history) and MBT has no X' window and a failed own-parameter walk-forward; per addendum 2 (R3) the cell is CLOSED as a seed-specific magnitude, kept as a one-line WATCH note (value: tens of dollars a week at 3 lots). Rows in the ledger as FIND_C_LRSFU_MBT / _BTC; n_tested +15 each.
CORRECTION (CHAR-C, same hour, to the paragraph above): the per-seed median joint z of MBT seed 6 is 1.4 (not 1.6), and the sign replicates in 10 of 10 NEW seeds (5 MBT seeds 3-7, 5 BTC seeds 0-4), not 13; the verdict is unchanged (median MBT 1.80 and BTC 1.44 vs the pre-stated 2.0).

### CHAR-B plain sentences (GC, CL, ZN, ZB; 2026-11-06)
- GC: the thing it does is revert at 5-30 min in London/asia/close (and a 15 min fade in NY-PM) with room 7-16x cost in every session, but the 1-min reversion is mostly last-print noise; the fade books that fit it are positive in 2026 (145 of 720 cells, 41 tolerant) and none survives: family p >= .59, 2013-2023 negative, so it does not clear (REGIME at best).
- CL: it reverts weakly at 5 min and 30 min in the close, bounces off prior-day levels in asia only as a break-through, with room 5-15x cost; the books that fit it (fade, level break) are positive in 5 of 500 cells tolerant and none clears the family test (best p .38); EIA, expiry-week, Monday-reopen and 14:30 windows show no continuation or reversal beyond random days.
- ZN: it reverts hard at 5-60 min in every NY session (VR(30) 0.57, prior-day/IB bounce 58-64%) with room only 1.2-2.8x cost, so the cost eats it: all 420 map-generated cells lose in HOLD, 0 tolerant; the learned rules reach p_family .22 at best; does not clear.
- ZB: the same picture (reverting at 5-30 min, level bounces 54-59%, room 2.2-3.2x cost); all 502 cells lose in HOLD, 0 tolerant, LRS best p_family .18; does not clear.
Common reading: rate futures revert at horizons shorter than their cost can pay; GC and CL show recent positive fades that are 2026-only. No book from the map for these four reaches the bar; nothing goes live.

## DS3_FIND 6J and NKD on the 1-SECOND path (CHAR-C, lead follow-up, addendum 3 in DECISIONS.md filed first)
Tapes found: /mnt/l2/l2vast/ds3/discover_data/{6J,NKD}_ohlcv1s_20230401_20261031.dbn.zst (+ NKD_trades_..., 1-min top-ups to 10-31), SPEND.json item LEAN_20261103 ($61.32; my earlier statement that no 1-s tape existed was wrong: I had searched instr_1s and data/databento only). 6J 12.56M 1-s bars, NKD 2.72M (seconds with prints only), 2023-04-02..2026-10-30, 15 instrument ids each.
Calibration 1 (data): the 1-s tape aggregated to 1-minute equals the owned 1-minute bars exactly (open/high/low/close equal in 100.0% of 1.23M 6J and 0.83M NKD matched minutes, max close difference 0 ticks, 0 minutes of the 1-min series missing from the 1-s tape): same exchange data, so the 1-min map on 2023-26 is unchanged.
Calibration 2 (simulation, gate weekly corr >= .90): every stage-1 cell re-simulated on the 1-s path (entry at the first print at/after the next-minute open, stop-first within a second, evaluator costs; time-stop rule corrected for 1-s bars, sim1s.py) vs the 1-min evaluator simulation, weekly equal-risk P&L correlation 2023-04..2026-10: 6J 468 cells median 0.994, 10th percentile 0.963, 96.2% of cells >= .90; NKD 132 cells median 0.995, 10th percentile 0.970, 100% >= .90. Calibrated: formal 1-s verdicts are admissible for both.
Books on the 1-s path (family null re-drawn with the 1-s simulator, random-side 100 worlds over all cells, per-world maxima /mnt/l2/l2vast/find_c/s1/nullmax_1s_<SYM>.npy): 6J tolerant cells 7 (1-min screen 15), smallest family p 0.21 (null max 95th percentile 0.36 vs best real z 0.28; the 1-min screen's p 0.01 cell was a zero-slip-target artefact and is p>0.2 on the full-cost path); evaluate() on the 1-s path for 13 cells (6 original finalists + top tolerant): all FAIL (tolerant gate on PRE/X'/NOW-ONLY, placebo p_book 0.37-0.68, family p 1.00, walk-forward not positive). NKD tolerant cells 0 (1-min screen 8), smallest family p 0.52 (best z 0.17), evaluate() on the 5 finalists: all FAIL (X' -353 to -660 per week, placebo p_book 0.42-0.64, family p 0.52-1.00). Nothing clears on the 1-s path either; the 1-min conclusions stand. Ledger programs FIND_C_1S_6J / FIND_C_1S_NKD (600 rows); frames /mnt/l2/l2vast/instr2_trades/<SYM>/FIND1S_*.parquet.
Sub-minute map (map_1s.py, 1-s grid, 5/15/30/60/300 s lag correlation by session, within-year shuffled-day null 300 worlds, family = horizons x sessions): 6J is strongly negative at 5-60 seconds in every session (5 s: -0.046 pre to -0.076 close; 15 s: -0.032 to -0.093, close -0.093; 30 s -0.024 to -0.090; 60 s -0.028 to -0.076; p_family .003) and mostly gone by 300 s (only NY-PM -0.026 and the close -0.033 keep p_family .003): this is the last-print bid-ask bounce measured directly, i.e. the 1-min reversion of the page is a bounce plus a small genuine 5-30 minute remainder. NKD has almost no sub-minute structure (|corr| <= 0.037; 5 s: +0.001 to +0.005, positive, i.e. thin-print continuation; pre-NY hour -0.016 to -0.034 at 15-60 s, p_family .003-.007; the close +0.037 at 300 s, p_family .003): its tape is too thin to carry a sub-minute book (median 1-s presence far below one print per second, cap 0.2-0.8 contracts per 5 minutes).
**6J and NKD on the 1-second path: same answer as at 1 minute - the reversion is real but is bid-ask bounce plus a sliver, no book clears, and the 1-s family p of the best 6J cell moves from 0.01 (screen) to 0.21 (full costs).**

Summary: plateau of one family (slow Keltner flips, Asia), positive 2022, 2024-26, MBT = BTCM, corr to NQ stack about 0, walk-forward positive in 3 of 4 refit rules, fresh-world count p 0.005 and max-stat p 0.03-0.09 CONDITIONAL on the selected region; unconditional full-grid max-stat p 0.13; fees take 0.03-0.06 R (t 1.1-1.9); cap-bound size $8-15 per week; label WATCH, not a lead.

## RV (relative-value spread) books - CHAR-B, 2026-11-06. Shadow only, no PASS claims, nothing live.
Prereg sha fa5c5c13.. + addendum1 (DECISIONS). Spread = causal beta-hedged pseudo-price, integer hedge unit (a:b contracts), BOTH legs' round trips paid (PROXY-STD fees, 1 tick in + 2 ticks stop per leg), close-path stops/targets, equal-$1,200 risk units, cap >= 1 unit, roll days excluded; frozen rule p_family <= .05 AND room_RV > 2. Evaluator instr2_eval (placebos shift5/side/days x100 on a spread-path re-simulator, family max-stat over ALL cells of the pair, own-parameter walk-forward, stack V0/V2 carry-dropped). Data end 2026-10-16 (common end of the legs). ### RV plain sentences (CHAR-B, 2026-11-06)
- ES-RTY: the beta-hedged spread reverts at 5-60 min outside NY-AM (VR(30) 0.87, nyam/nypm room 4-5x the 167 two-leg cost), but the 378 fade cells lose in the median (HOLD -$1,348/wk): both legs' cost eats the reversion; 14 cells positive in L6M and HOLD, none passes the tolerant gate, best family p .14. Does not clear.
- ES-YM: reverts at 5-30 min (VR(30) 0.74) with room 3.5-4.3x cost in NY-AM/PM; all 288 cells lose in HOLD (median -$1,426/wk). Does not clear.
- RTY-YM: reverts at 5 min (VR(30) 0.92), room 5-7x the 99 two-leg cost in NY; 19 of 342 cells pass the tolerant gate but the family p is .97 and only 7 are positive in both recent windows (median HOLD -$730/wk). Does not clear.
- ZN-ZB: the 3:1 duration-hedged spread is almost pure last-print noise (5-min AC -0.2 to -0.35, VR(30) 0.19) and its room is only 0.6-0.8x the $254 two-leg cost, so the frozen rule generates no book. Does not clear.
- 6E-6J: reverts at 5-15 min in NY-PM/close (VR(30) 0.65), room 3.2-4.4x the 97 cost; all 546 cells lose in HOLD (median -$1,508/wk). Does not clear.
- GC-CL: correlation 0.04-0.07 with a sign that flips by year; no stable hedge, no spread. 
Common reading: every spread reverts in the tape but by less than two legs' costs; the map's reversion is mostly asynchronous last-print noise in the two legs.

## REPAIRED-FIND (CHAR-A slice, 2026-11-06T06:17Z): T-FIND books and WATCH cells re-run on repaired bars. Method: 1-min = CHAR-C repaired copies /mnt/l2/l2vast/find_c/cache_c/<SYM>.npz (DATA_BAD_PRINTS.md rule); 1-s = my scan of instr_1s with the same rule (neighbours within 3 s, sigma = 1.4826 x block median |close change| of the previous 3,600 bars), clamped copies in /mnt/l2/l2vast/ds3/out/find/instr1s_repaired/. Same signals, specs, costs, seeds, 100 placebo worlds; originals untouched.
**Verdict: no label, family result or frozen spec moves; only marginal gate flips and learned-rule retrains.**

### 1-s bad-print scan (2023-08..2026-10-16)

| NQ | 40.1M | 34.4M | 4,714 | 0.014% | 203 |
| RTY | 21.0M | 15.4M | 1,094 | 0.007% | 122 |
Some flagged bars on a prints-only 1-s tape may be genuine one-lot spikes; the repair clamps into the 10-sigma band, so it is conservative.

### Map statistics on the repaired 1-min bars (descriptive pages not recomputed in full)
ES / NQ / RTY / YM: max |change| of the pooled lag correlation over horizons 1/5/15/30 min x 6 sessions = 0.0004 / 0.0014 / 0.0050 / 0.0011 (all at the 1-minute pre-market cell); max |change| of a variance ratio 0.0008 / 0.0017 / 0.0037 / 0.0013; max |change| of a level-reaction / continuation / gap / day-type event rate 0.0069 / 0.0047 / 0.0086 / 0.0077. The Monday legs of ES and NQ (label-day open to 16:55 close) are identical to the last digit (NQ Monday FULL mean 17.54 bps, sd 120.57, 2023+ mean 31.52; ES 12.71 / 97.85 / 23.62), so WATCH_MONDAY_LONG_SPEC.md and AMENDMENT_1 stand.

### 1-min finalists re-run on repaired bars: 126 book cells (top-30 by min(L6M,HOLD) + all tolerant cells per instrument, ES/RTY/YM) and 18 learned-rule cells (top-6 per instrument, instr2/lrs.py)
* Books (126): median |change| in L6M / HOLD / PRE / X' = 0 / 0 / 0 / 0 $/wk; maximum |change| L6M 76, HOLD 206 $/wk; 37 books have a different trade count (max 6 trades). Sign flips: L6M 0, HOLD 2 (all marginal cells near zero, listed below); tolerant-gate flips 4.
* Learned rules (18; trees retrained on repaired features, so the trade set changes): maximum |change| L6M 831, HOLD 477 $/wk; sign flips L6M 8, HOLD 5; tolerant flips 0. This is the sensitivity of a retrained tree to 0.003% of its input bars, not a result.
* The four tolerant flips are YM NY-PM 5-minute fades at the gate's edge (PRE -216 -> -215 $/wk against the -0.5 MDE line; L6M and HOLD unchanged); the best-family-p cells of ES, RTY and YM that are in the finalist list (the YM VR NY-PM 5-min z1.5 f0.5 cell, p .15, is) do not change tolerance or sign.
